In organ failures, especially kidney and liver failure are on the agenda, and many people are waiting for organs. The removal of organs from a living person is carried out by taking one of the double organs, which would not put the donor's life at risk. An organ transplant from the cadaver is possible, depending on the consent of its relatives and by giving an organ before death. The number of hemodialysis patients in Turkey is around 44,000 per year due to severe kidney failure. Cadaver transplants are of great importance to our citizens who are waiting for organ transplants due to kidney failure and liver failure. As a result of kidney failure, the average life expectancy of five years is 33 per cent, and the average life expectancy of five years is 90 per cent if the same patient is given a kidney transplant. With this treatment, the quality of patient life is both increasing and growing significantly, so transplanting is trying not to die, and dialysis is trying not to. But in our country, unfortunately, only a quarter of the transports are from the cadaver. In European countries, 80 per cent of organ transplants are from cadavers. So far, many patients have died due to the absence of organ donors. Although we have many organ transplant centers and many organ transplant surgeons in our country, there are not enough organ transplants since we don't have a cadaver transplant. The other important reason is that brain death cases are not reported. That's why many of our citizens have lost their lives in the order of organ transplants. Therefore, the use of brain death phenomena and reporting of them are important for both saving the lives of those waiting on the transplant lists and preventing organ trafficking. Public workers have the right to strike in the ILO Convention 87. However, the right to strike cannot be decided only by the text of the contracts. The ILO control bodies have acknowledged that the strike is a basic right for all workers to protect and improve their economic and social interests based on the three separate articles of Article 87. According to the expert committee, the right to strike has three grounds stemming from Contract 87: the first is the third clause of the contract that allows unions to organise and develop their activities and action programmes; the second is the second article of the article in the article, which stipulates that national legislation does not harm or harm the guarantees envisioned in the contract, or does not apply to them, and the third is the article in the contract, which aims to defend and improve the interests of employees and employers. Because a general ban on the right to strike deprives trade unions of the rights and resources of the issues mentioned, which is incompatible with the principle of union freedom. On another occasion, the ILO Freedom of Organization Committee also assessed: "Greev's rights should not be limited to disputes that can be resolved through the signing of a collective contract, workers and organisations should be able to express their reactions to economic and social issues that affect the interests of their members in a broader scope." "The unions should be able to resort to protest strikes, especially to criticize the economic and social policies of a government. In addition, the general ban on solidarity strikes can lead to abuse and when it comes to the use of the strike rights, it is justified that the work be slowed down, the occupation of workplaces and the taking of residence strikes in the workplace, but that these actions do not cease to be peaceful." It's been said. Democratic action and activities for human rights are also guaranteed under international agreements. As a matter of fact, the European Court of Human Rights ruled that collective acts for the union, the collective contract and the right to strike are a human right. In this context, the proposed law, which is a coup to the Union of Air-Business, concerned the 14,000 Turkish Airlines employees who have found 50,000 with their families, must be dropped from parliament's agenda. It is not possible to accept a law in the aviation business, which has been in the air services for 18 months, which is a coup to the collective contract of employment process.
